1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any stratigraphy module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 103. The projective resolution has length at most 463 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the stratigraphy space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 103 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 463. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The stratigraphy operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 739. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 16 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 739 + O(1/n^16). Summing over all modes and applying the stratigraphy structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
